# Institution shortlists

Research snapshot: 2026-10-18

122 department/award profiles across all 21 requested course forms.

## How to use this guide

- A selective, AI-assisted research shortlist, not a complete directory, accreditation audit or certification that an institution meets every standard. Recommendations mean priority for investigation, not a ranking.
- Each profile is one specific campus, department, award and admission route. MD/MS, DNB and direct six-year DrNB are separate. INI-CET is shown separately from NEET-PG and its counselling routes.
- Direct six-year paediatric/plastic surgery is post-MBBS entry through the applicable NEET-PG/MCC process, distinct from post-MS NEET-SS. Verify exact current course, duration, training rotations, exit rules and offered seats.
- Institutional descriptions, procedure totals and faculty rosters are self-reports with dates and scopes. Hospital beds, accreditation and institute rankings do not establish a resident’s actual case load or primary-operator exposure.
- No resident-culture or toxicity score is inferred. An ICC or grievance policy establishes a process on paper, not the lived experience. Verify independently with residents across training years.
- Fees and stipends are joined only by exact campus ID (and exact course for tuition). Original periods, training years, unresolved frequency and conflicting disclosures remain visible. Missing data is not zero; there is no automatic three-times-annual estimate.
- Supplied 2025 and 2026 matrix labels are retained. Original seats, mid-counselling additions and 2026 seats are different fields. These files do not cover every state/private route or establish current vacancies.
- Travel map links are search/planning aids, not verified distances, fastest railheads or scheduled Hyderabad flights. Confirm the reporting campus, passenger airport, road journey and actual train/flight timetable.
- Language notes are planning prompts. Telugu, Hindi and English are the applicant baseline. Ask how the department supports regional-language learning and consent, history-taking and counselling; no arbitrary fluency timeline is assumed.
- No admission prediction is made without rank, category, domicile and the current counselling rules. Recheck recognition/accreditation, eligibility, fees, stipend, bonds and service obligations before choosing.

## Resident checks

- Speak separately with women residents and at least one resident from each year; ask what changed recently and date each answer.
- Request actual last-month duty rosters, post-call rest, leave, supervision overnight, workload and stipend-payment dates.
- Check women’s hostel availability, late-duty transport, changing/rest areas, reporting support and the actual ICC/grievance process.
- Request branch-specific trainee logs: primary operator versus assistant/observer, reporting responsibility, procedures, rotations and competition with fellows/other trainees.
- Read the complete bond, penalty, notice period, discontinuation rules, tuition schedule, refundable deposits and compulsory hostel/mess charges.
- Confirm current faculty and accreditation at the exact campus, exam outcomes with batch denominators, thesis support and protected academic time.

## Paediatric Surgery — direct six years

**Editorial shortlisting view:** Editorial view: start with KKCTH and Sir Ganga Ram for the available programme-plus-department evidence; compare Wadia, SRCC, Aster MIMS, Narayana Health City and Lilavati after obtaining real resident case allocation. None is certified to meet all requested standards. For this unusually long commitment, supervision, major neonatal surgery and resident welfare should outweigh brand or proximity.

- Six-year commitment: obtain rotations, assessment milestones, exit/transfer rules and whether any separate intermediate qualification is awarded.
- Require neonatal and infant major surgery, paediatric urology, oncology/thoracic cases, paediatric ICU and anaesthesia support.
- Compare the last three cohorts’ primary-operator logbooks, completion/attrition and exit-exam outcomes; a referral-centre name is insufficient.
- Direct post-MBBS six-year NBEMS training is DrNB via NEET-PG; legacy matrices may call it DNB. Do not substitute a three-year post-MS programme through NEET-SS.
- No NEET-PG/INI-CET rank, reservation category or domicile eligibility has been supplied; these are training-evidence candidates, not predictions of admission.
- Keep NEET-PG/MCC, state counselling and INI-CET in separate choice lists; supplied matrix rows are dated listings, not current vacancies.
- All fee levels are retained. Compare all-in annual and total cost, stipend and any bond for the exact seat category, rather than excluding by an unstated budget.

### NEET-PG / other specified counselling

#### Kanchi Kamakoti CHILDS Trust Hospital, Nungambakkam — Direct 6-year DrNB

Chennai, Tamil Nadu · Primary · Private / charitable hospital

**Research priority:** Priority to investigate. **Route:** NEET-PG / MCC.

**Programme evidence:** NBEMS official portal checked 18 October 2026 lists 2 accredited broad/super-specialty seat(s), valid up to Dec-2026, for the exact campus and Paediatric Surgery (Direct 6 Years Course). Counselling inclusion and renewal for the intended admission cycle remain separate checks. https://accr.natboard.edu.in/online_user/frontpage.php?v=4 | https://natboard.edu.in/dnbsuper

- A particularly useful first investigation because both the explicit six-year route and a branch-specific procedure-volume claim are available; independently validate case allocation and culture. https://kkcth.org/academics | https://kkcth.org/pediatric-surgery-and-urology
- Worth investigating as a department-specific programme verified in the NBEMS live accreditation list at this exact campus; this is not an outcome ranking or a guarantee that training standards are met. https://accr.natboard.edu.in/online_user/frontpage.php?v=4
- Unlike a hospital-brand-only lead, the official record names faculty against this exact programme; use that to identify supervisors to investigate. https://accr.natboard.edu.in/online_user/frontpage.php?v=4

**Department:** Official department describes neonatal, oncological, gastrointestinal/hepatobiliary, urological, thoracic and laparoscopic work. The academy explicitly identifies the post-MBBS six-year pathway and regular classes/mock exams. https://kkcth.org/pediatric-surgery-and-urology | https://kkcth.org/academics

**Patient Load:** Institution reports >1,200 paediatric operations per year across the listed domains. Undated self-report; no audited year, major/minor split or per-resident primary-operator share was supplied. Request those before judging surgical exposure. https://kkcth.org/pediatric-surgery-and-urology

**Faculty:** NBEMS programme-specific faculty snapshot, 18 October 2026, includes Dr. S. Balagopal; Dr. Priya Ramachandran; Dr. Lakhmi Sundararajan. Reconfirm who is full time, still employed, available on call and personally teaching residents; the portal warns that faculty status can change. https://accr.natboard.edu.in/online_user/frontpage.php?v=4

**Established:** Institution and department establishment years were not established from consulted sources; verify separately. Accreditation validity is not the department’s foundation year. 

**Resident Welfare:** Not independently established. Ask current female residents privately about actual duty hours, post-call rest, leave, stipend delays, hostel availability, night transport, harassment/grievance access and retaliation concerns. Reputation and accreditation do not establish a non-toxic culture. 

**Cost:** Current all-in course cost and actual credited stipend were not established from consulted programme sources. Obtain annual tuition for every year, hostel/mess, deposits, exam fees, deductions and any service/discontinuation liability in writing. 

**Language:** Editorial planning assumption, not a measured departmental language audit: budget time to learn clinical Tamil and ask how new residents obtain safe communication support. Applicant speaks Telugu, Hindi and English; English academic teaching should not be equated with English-speaking patients. 

**Travel:** Campus-specific railway access, scheduled-passenger airport access and road distances were not established from consulted sources. Measure the actual hostel-to-station/airport trip and verify current Hyderabad connections before ranking; a hospital name or an old-airport-road address is not an airport-access measurement. 

**Supplied matrices:** Supplied-file seats, not live vacancies. Quotas and 2025 original/additions/2026 stay separate. Blank / “No source row” means no row for that component, not zero seats.

**Verify next:**

- Accreditation displayed only through December 2026: require renewal evidence for any later session; do not assume six-year training or a later intake is authorised from this date alone.
- Visit the actual teaching campus and review a recent resident logbook rather than a marketing hospital tour.
- Speak separately with at least two current residents from different years, including women, without faculty present.
- Confirm the current admission-session programme, category/eligibility, fees, stipend, hostel and bond before choice filling.
- Verify the full six-year surgical rotation plan, intermediate/exit assessment rules, transfer/discontinuation options and financial liability; do not assume a separate MS qualification is automatically awarded midway.
- Ask for major index-operation counts per resident, including emergency/night access and the division of cases with fellows and consultants.
- Confirm neonatal emergency surgery, paediatric urology, oncology, thoracic work, neonatal ICU and paediatric anaesthesia exposure, not only hernias/day-care cases.

**Evidence gaps:**

- Independent resident-culture and female-hostel/night-transport evidence is missing.
- No verified campus-to-railhead/airport road distance or Hyderabad timetable was obtained.
- No independent six-year completion, exit-pass, attrition or post-training placement outcomes were obtained.
- Institutional academics page says one seat/year while current NBEMS accreditation says two; reconcile the actual counselling intake, resident denominator and teaching capacity.

[Campus map](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Kanchi+Kamakoti+CHILDS+Trust+Hospital%2C+Nungambakkam%2C+Chennai%2C+Tamil+Nadu) · [Plan from Hyderabad](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=Hyderabad%2C+Telangana&destination=Kanchi+Kamakoti+CHILDS+Trust+Hospital%2C+Nungambakkam%2C+Chennai%2C+Tamil+Nadu)

## Plastic & Reconstructive Surgery — direct six years

**Editorial shortlisting view:** Editorial view: investigate Apollo Jubilee Hills first for home-metro practical fit, then compare Sir Ganga Ram, Baby Memorial, Aster MIMS, Aster CMI, Manipal Old Airport Road and SIMS for actual reconstructive/microsurgical access. This is an investigation order, not a quality ranking. Aesthetic-heavy advertising alone is a poor basis for a six-year commitment.

- Verify a balanced six-year programme: acute burns, hand/trauma, cleft, reconstructive microsurgery, replantation and aesthetic work.
- Ask whether a resident can perform index operations under supervision and how fellows/consultants compete for cases.
- Examine intermediate/exit assessment, transfer/attrition and discontinuation liability before committing six years.
- Direct post-MBBS six-year NBEMS training is DrNB via NEET-PG; legacy matrices may call it DNB. Do not substitute a three-year post-MS programme through NEET-SS.
- No NEET-PG/INI-CET rank, reservation category or domicile eligibility has been supplied; these are training-evidence candidates, not predictions of admission.
- Keep NEET-PG/MCC, state counselling and INI-CET in separate choice lists; supplied matrix rows are dated listings, not current vacancies.
- All fee levels are retained. Compare all-in annual and total cost, stipend and any bond for the exact seat category, rather than excluding by an unstated budget.

## Pathology

**Editorial shortlisting view:** Editorial view: for your Hyderabad base, personally inspect Osmania and the Apollo Jubilee Hills/Basavatarakam DNB departments early, while comparing the documented breadth at CMC, KMC Manipal and JIPMER/AIIMS. A nearby supportive department with reliable slide feedback can be preferable to a costly or famous service with little resident diagnostic ownership. Basavatarakam Pathology requires an immediate renewal-cycle check.

- See the last 12 months of surgical specimens, FNAC/cytology, bone marrows, autopsies and subspecialty mix; compare with total residents across all batches.
- Confirm residents gross specimens, preview slides, perform supervised FNAC/marrow procedures and receive signed diagnostic feedback rather than merely dispatching samples.
- Check functioning IHC/flow/molecular testing, external haematology/blood-bank rotations where needed, thesis mentoring and protected exam preparation.
- Distinguish broad MD/DNB preparation from an oncology-heavy service. Ask about non-neoplastic cases, haematology, autopsies and external rotations.
- Record case volumes as specimens/procedures per reporting year, then personal resident exposure. Hospital beds/OPD and machine lists are not pathology workload.
- MD and three-year post-MBBS DNB are different counselling entries; post-diploma columns are not extra MBBS-entry seats.
- AIIMS/JIPMER stay in a separate INI-CET list; CMC requires exact Tamil Nadu/current institutional admission checks.
- Current NBEMS listing is not a counselling vacancy or an independent culture assessment.

- Broad diagnostic services, transplant/molecular pathology and MDTs are expressly described. https://jipmer.edu.in/department/pathology/general-info

**Department:** Broad histology/cytology/haematology, IHC, flow cytometry, renal transplant pathology, molecular services and EQAS/MDTs are described. https://jipmer.edu.in/department/pathology/general-info

**Patient Load:** Not established from consulted sources; obtain the latest department annual counts and anonymised resident logbooks, with denominators and reporting year.

**Established:** Department started 1964, according to official general information. https://jipmer.edu.in/department/pathology/general-info

**Resident Welfare:** July 2026 prospectus provides separate male/female hostels, limited rooms subject to availability, primarily for non-Puducherry candidates; HRA is not granted while in hostel. These are accommodation rules, not independent proof of supportive department culture. Ask female residents about night access, duty/rest, leave, grievance handling and timely pay. https://jipmer.edu.in/sites/default/files/3.%20%20Prospectus%20Part%20-%20B%20%28JIPMER%29.pdf

**Cost:** July 2026 MD/MS fee table: tuition₹2,200/year plus₹9,000/year learning resources,₹110/year corpus and₹1,500/year student information (₹12,810 recurring academic charges/year);₹5,000 admission and₹150 ID are one-time, plus₹3,000 refundable caution deposit. Hostel/living costs are separate. Residency contract states Level 10 entry basic₹56,100/month plus applicable allowances, not take-home. Check subsequent-session revisions. https://jipmer.edu.in/sites/default/files/3.%20%20Prospectus%20Part%20-%20B%20%28JIPMER%29.pdf

**Language:** Editorial fit: plan to learn clinical Tamil for local histories/consent. Ask residents how Telugu/Hindi/English are used with referral patients, what interpreter support exists and the expected learning period; do not assume English-only patient work. 

**Travel:** Not established from consulted sources; verify the exact training campus, practical railway station, passenger airport, Hyderabad connections and safe late-arrival transport. No travel time or distance has been estimated. 

**Supplied matrices:** No exact campus-and-course match joined in the supplied files; this does not mean zero seats.

- Check functioning IHC/flow/molecular testing, external haematology/blood-bank rotations where needed, thesis mentoring and protected exam preparation.

## Radiation Oncology

**Editorial shortlisting view:** Editorial view: investigate TMC, CMC and JIPMER alongside the Hyderabad departments, especially verified Basavatarakam and Apollo Jubilee Hills DNB options. A functioning broad programme with regular contour review, physics support and personally supervised brachytherapy should outrank a machine-brand promise. RCC/Kidwai remain serious leads but require primary department data; Apollo Proton is a specialised alternative only after broad training is verified.

- Ask for machines actually delivering treatment, downtime/backup and source-replacement records; verify routine CT simulation, planning workstations and contouring/plan-review access per resident.
- Compare new patient starts, treated fractions and brachytherapy procedures separately; ask what residents personally contour, plan, insert and review under supervision.
- Check adequate physics/QA teaching, cervical/interstitial brachytherapy, site-specific tumour boards, supportive-care experience, treatment breaks and patient follow-up.
- Ask about emotionally difficult consultations, palliation, supervision after hours and time for physics/exam study.
- Assess cervical/interstitial brachytherapy exposure and planned rotations before selecting a highly specialised technology centre.
- Radiation Oncology/Radiotherapy is distinct from Radiodiagnosis and Nuclear Medicine.
- Do not interpret generic proton mentions on a departmental description as evidence of an operating proton service.
- TMC is listed here through NEET-PG/MCC evidence; do not infer INI-CET simply from central status.
- Verify exact accredited campus: multi-campus cancer brands can have different programmes, equipment and rotations.

## General Surgery

**Editorial shortlisting view:** Editorial view: use Osmania as a local conditional baseline to visit; investigate KMC Manipal, JIPMER, CMC, Sir Ganga Ram, Deenanath Mangeshkar and Yashoda Malakpet by operative autonomy and supervision. Keep JIPMER INI-CET separate. KMC/JIPMER department totals are useful questions, not proof that an individual trainee operates enough.

- Compare emergency and elective index operations performed as primary operator, not only assisted or hospital totals.
- Check open and laparoscopic fundamentals, endoscopy, ICU, trauma, anatomy/skills lab and supervised progression.
- Ask how work is distributed among junior/senior residents, super-specialty trainees and consultants and whether general surgery loses operative cases to specialty services.
- No NEET-PG/INI-CET rank, reservation category or domicile eligibility has been supplied; these are training-evidence candidates, not predictions of admission.
- Keep NEET-PG/MCC, state counselling and INI-CET in separate choice lists; supplied matrix rows are dated listings, not current vacancies.
- All fee levels are retained. Compare all-in annual and total cost, stipend and any bond for the exact seat category, rather than excluding by an unstated budget.

**Established:** AIIMS New Delhi was established by Act of Parliament in 1956. The specific department establishment year was not established. https://www.aiims.edu/index.php/en/academic_section_courses

**Resident Welfare:** Not independently established. Ask current female residents privately about actual duty hours, post-call rest, leave, stipend delays, hostel availability, night transport, harassment/grievance access and retaliation concerns. Reputation and accreditation do not establish a non-toxic culture. 

**Cost:** Current all-in course cost and actual credited stipend were not established from consulted programme sources. Obtain annual tuition for every year, hostel/mess, deposits, exam fees, deductions and any service/discontinuation liability in writing. 

**Language:** Editorial planning assumption, not a measured departmental language audit: Hindi may offer a practical starting advantage; verify the actual multi-state referral mix and communication support. Applicant speaks Telugu, Hindi and English; English academic teaching should not be equated with English-speaking patients. 

**Travel:** Campus-specific railway access, scheduled-passenger airport access and road distances were not established from consulted sources. Measure the actual hostel-to-station/airport trip and verify current Hyderabad connections before ranking; a hospital name or an old-airport-road address is not an airport-access measurement. 

**Supplied matrices:** No exact campus-and-course match joined in the supplied files; this does not mean zero seats.

**Verify next:**

- Visit the actual teaching campus and review a recent resident logbook rather than a marketing hospital tour.
- Speak separately with at least two current residents from different years, including women, without faculty present.
- Confirm the current admission-session programme, category/eligibility, fees, stipend, hostel and bond before choice filling.
- Check the current AIIMS New Delhi institute-specific Part B and final INI-CET specialty/category seat roster; JIPMER pay, fees and hostel terms do not apply by inference.
- Confirm actual duty location and hostel commute: AIIMS has multiple clinical blocks/campuses; brand-level airport distances are insufficient.

**Evidence gaps:**

- Independent resident-culture and female-hostel/night-transport evidence is missing.
- No verified campus-to-railhead/airport road distance or Hyderabad timetable was obtained.

## Paediatrics

**Editorial shortlisting view:** Editorial view: begin with local Osmania (programme listing only; needs deeper validation) and NBEMS-verified Rainbow/Yashoda campuses, then compare CMC, JIPMER, KMC Manipal and Amrita for documented clinical breadth. Prefer balanced common paediatrics plus neonatal/critical-care ownership over a subspecialty-heavy brand alone; Amrita’s verified annual tuition makes cost a major separate decision.

- General paediatric OPD/IPD, age spectrum and common diseases as well as referrals; counts by year and new/repeat visits.
- Resident-owned emergency stabilisation, newborn resuscitation, NICU/PICU rotations and procedures; clarify what is reserved for fellows.
- Bed occupancy, nursing and overnight consultant availability; duty/rest and post-call relief.
- Community/immunisation/developmental clinics and family counselling in the patient’s language.
- NEET-PG/MCC, state counselling and INI-CET are separate routes; verify the actual programme and current-cycle seat/category.
- DNB accredited broad-specialty capacity, post-diploma capacity and live counselling availability are separate quantities.
- No rank, reservation category or domicile eligibility was supplied; this is training due diligence, not an admission prediction.

**Established:** Paediatric outpatient services began in 1962 and MD Paediatrics in 1967, per official centre history; these are separate from institute founding. https://pgimer.edu.in/PGIMER_PORTAL/PGIMERPORTAL/GlobalPages/JSP/Page_Data.jsp?dep_id=19

**Resident Welfare:** Official ICC page publishes complaint arrangements and links February 2026 reconstitution orders. This documents policy, not a safe/non-toxic department. Verify confidentiality, resident experience, duty/rest, women’s hostel and late-night transport with present trainees. https://pgimer.edu.in/PGIMER_PORTAL/PGIMERPORTAL/GlobalPages/JSP/Page_Data.jsp?dep_id=5874

**Cost:** Exact current fee and stipend package not established from consulted sources. All fee levels are retained; compare tuition, hostel/mess, taxes, bonds and exit penalties before ranking. 

**Language:** Editorial fit: ask current residents how much Hindi/Punjabi and other referral-population languages is needed for histories, consent and counselling, and whether supervised language induction and translation support are available. English teaching does not establish English-only patient care. 

**Travel:** Campus-specific railway-station and passenger-airport distances were not corroborated in consulted sources. Verify the named campus route and late-night last-mile journey before using proximity to rank it.

- Confirm exact campus, current recognised/accredited award and counselling seat; source listings are not admission chances.
- Speak privately with at least two current residents, preferably women in different years, about duty/rest, leave, stipend delays, supervision and grievance handling.
- Inspect female hostel eligibility, washrooms, late-night campus-to-hostel transport and emergency escort arrangements rather than relying on city reputation.
- Obtain a written all-year fee, stipend, hostel/mess, bond and exit-penalty schedule for your exact counselling category.
- Obtain current official INI-CET Part B and session seat distribution; old PGIMER admission pages describe a separate pre-INI exam and must not be used as current exam instructions.
- Treat Chandigarh as an explicit outside-list comparison and plan Hyderabad travel using your actual hostel/campus; passenger-airport/rail last-mile distances were not corroborated here.

**Evidence gaps:**

- Current INI-CET session seats and programme rules, full-time faculty, dated department workload, final costs and campus travel remain unverified.

## Ophthalmology

**Editorial shortlisting view:** Editorial view: compare dedicated eye-hospital programmes (Aravind Madurai MS/Noombal DNB, Sankara Nethralaya Chennai and Sankara Eye Guntur) against broader medical-college MS options (KMC, JIPMER, CMC and a conditional Osmania lead). Cataract autonomy and supervised complication experience should drive the decision alongside subspecialty breadth. Do not infer a current LV Prasad DNB seat from its reputation: it was not established in the consulted live list.

- Require year-wise MSICS/phaco primary-operator numbers and supervised complication management; check the wet-lab access timetable.
- Examine retina, glaucoma, cornea, paediatric, oculoplasty and emergency rotations, not cataract volume alone.
- Distinguish the exact campus and award: Aravind Madurai MS differs from Noombal DNB; Sankara Eye Guntur differs from Sankara Nethralaya Chennai.
- No NEET-PG/INI-CET rank, reservation category or domicile eligibility has been supplied; these are training-evidence candidates, not predictions of admission.
- Keep NEET-PG/MCC, state counselling and INI-CET in separate choice lists; supplied matrix rows are dated listings, not current vacancies.
- All fee levels are retained. Compare all-in annual and total cost, stipend and any bond for the exact seat category, rather than excluding by an unstated budget.

## ENT

**Editorial shortlisting view:** Editorial view: investigate local Apollo Jubilee Hills/Yashoda Secunderabad DNB and Osmania MS lead alongside KMC Manipal, JIPMER, CMC, Sir Ganga Ram and Deenanath Mangeshkar. Compare routine operating access and faculty stability before being impressed by a department’s advanced implant/skull-base portfolio.

- Require logged tympanoplasty/mastoid, FESS, airway and head-neck procedures as primary operator, with complications taught and supervised.
- Check temporal-bone dissection, endoscopic equipment, audiology/vestibular/voice/swallowing services and current faculty cover.
- Distinguish exposure to advanced skull-base/cochlear work from the actual supervised procedures available to an MS/DNB resident.
- No NEET-PG/INI-CET rank, reservation category or domicile eligibility has been supplied; these are training-evidence candidates, not predictions of admission.
- Keep NEET-PG/MCC, state counselling and INI-CET in separate choice lists; supplied matrix rows are dated listings, not current vacancies.
- All fee levels are retained. Compare all-in annual and total cost, stipend and any bond for the exact seat category, rather than excluding by an unstated budget.

## Dermatology

**Editorial shortlisting view:** Editorial view: keep Hyderabad MD leads and newly verified Lallaguda DNB on a local investigation list. CMC/JIPMER/Amrita and Somaiya provide more public clinical-service evidence; balance medical dermatology/STI/leprosy/pathology with supervised procedures. Lower clinical-language friction or laser inventory alone should not determine rank.

- Medical inpatient dermatology, emergency drug reactions/bullous disease and dermatopathology correlation, not a cosmetic-only clinic.
- STI and leprosy clinic case mix, paediatric/genetic/autoimmune disease and phototherapy access.
- Resident-performed biopsies/dermatosurgery/lasers with named supervision and procedure logs; consultant procedure counts are insufficient.
- General OPD counts separated into new/repeat patients, resident numbers, teaching time and language support for histories/sexual-health counselling.
- NEET-PG/MCC, state counselling and INI-CET are separate routes; verify the actual programme and current-cycle seat/category.
- DNB accredited broad-specialty capacity, post-diploma capacity and live counselling availability are separate quantities.
- No rank, reservation category or domicile eligibility was supplied; this is training due diligence, not an admission prediction.

## Radiodiagnosis

**Editorial shortlisting view:** Editorial view: compare local NIMS/Osmania MD leads and Yashoda DNB against the more detailed CMC/KMC/JIPMER/Amrita department evidence. KMC publishes modality throughput, but this is not resident exposure. Obtain individual ultrasound/reporting logs, rotation timetables and machine uptime before using fee or reputation as a proxy.

- Hands-on ultrasound and Doppler scans per resident, CT/MRI preliminary reporting and faculty feedback, not only scanner inventory.
- General/emergency, paediatric, neuro, body, musculoskeletal, breast and fluoroscopy rotations plus IR exposure with documented supervision.
- Modality uptime, PACS access and hours, radiographer support, shared workload among MD/DNB/fellows.
- Dosimetry, shielding, occupational health and documented pregnancy-duty arrangements; verify clinical-language support for consent and ultrasound.
- NEET-PG/MCC, state counselling and INI-CET are separate routes; verify the actual programme and current-cycle seat/category.
- DNB accredited broad-specialty capacity, post-diploma capacity and live counselling availability are separate quantities.
- No rank, reservation category or domicile eligibility was supplied; this is training due diligence, not an admission prediction.

#### JIPMER Puducherry — Radiodiagnosis — MD

Puducherry, Puducherry · Primary · Central / INI

**Research priority:** Priority to investigate. **Route:** INI-CET.

**Programme evidence:** MD programme explicitly established by the official department academic page and the official July 2026 INI-CET Part B; other sessions require their own seat matrix. https://jipmer.edu.in/department/radiodiagnosis/academic | https://jipmer.edu.in/sites/default/files/3.%20%20Prospectus%20Part%20-%20B%20%28JIPMER%29.pdf

- Editorial priority based on the documented programme and department features below; this is a due-diligence candidate, not certification that standards are met. https://jipmer.edu.in/department/radiodiagnosis/academic | https://jipmer.edu.in/sites/default/files/3.%20%20Prospectus%20Part%20-%20B%20%28JIPMER%29.pdf

**Department:** Services across five blocks include MRI, CT, ultrasound and image-guided work. Academic page describes monthly modality rotations and case discussions; verify current allocation and IR access. https://jipmer.edu.in/department/radiodiagnosis/academic | https://jipmer.edu.in/department/radiodiagnosis/general-info

**Patient Load:** Current annual or daily department modality throughput was not established. Equipment counts are not reporting/scanning opportunities per resident. https://jipmer.edu.in/department/radiodiagnosis/general-info

**Faculty:** Roster lists K. Nagarajan, A. Ramesh, S. Deepak Barathi and Sunitha V. C.; 2022 roster and 2025 general-information page disagree on HOD. Confirm the current HOD, full-time faculty and supervisors explicitly. https://jipmer.edu.in/department/radiodiagnosis/faculty-and-staff | https://jipmer.edu.in/department/radiodiagnosis/general-info

**Established:** Radiology department began 1961; Radiodiagnosis/Radiotherapy split in 1990. MD Radiodiagnosis began 1981, per academic page. https://jipmer.edu.in/department/radiodiagnosis/academic | https://jipmer.edu.in/department/radiodiagnosis/general-info

**Resident Welfare:** July 2026 Part B provides separate male/female resident hostels but says PG rooms are limited, subject to availability, primarily for non-Puducherry candidates and allotted first come first served. This establishes policy, not hostel entitlement or lived working culture. Verify women residents’ duty/rest, night transport, leave, stipend punctuality and confidential grievance support. https://jipmer.edu.in/sites/default/files/3.%20%20Prospectus%20Part%20-%20B%20%28JIPMER%29.pdf

**Cost:** July 2026 Part B specifies annual tuition ₹2,200 plus annual learning-resource ₹9,000, corpus ₹110 and student-information ₹1,500; one-time admission ₹5,000, ID ₹150 and refundable caution ₹3,000. Hostel/mess and deposits are additional. MD/MS junior-resident pay is Level 10 basic ₹56,100/month plus admissible allowances, not guaranteed take-home. These are session-specific published figures, subject to revision. https://jipmer.edu.in/sites/default/files/3.%20%20Prospectus%20Part%20-%20B%20%28JIPMER%29.pdf

**Language:** Editorial fit: ask current residents how much Tamil is needed for histories, consent and counselling, and whether supervised language induction and translation support are available. English teaching does not establish English-only patient care. 

**Travel:** Campus-specific railway-station and passenger-airport distances were not corroborated in consulted sources. Verify the named campus route and late-night last-mile journey before using proximity to rank it. 

**Supplied matrices:** No exact campus-and-course match joined in the supplied files; this does not mean zero seats.

**Verify next:**

- Confirm exact campus, current recognised/accredited award and counselling seat; source listings are not admission chances.
- Speak privately with at least two current residents, preferably women in different years, about duty/rest, leave, stipend delays, supervision and grievance handling.
- Inspect female hostel eligibility, washrooms, late-night campus-to-hostel transport and emergency escort arrangements rather than relying on city reputation.
- Obtain a written all-year fee, stipend, hostel/mess, bond and exit-penalty schedule for your exact counselling category.
- INI-CET and the institute Part B/seat list are separate from NEET-PG/MCC; older annual intake is not the current session seat count.
- Check personal dosimetry, radiation-safety training, occupational health and documented pregnancy-related duty arrangements; do not infer policy from available machines.

**Evidence gaps:**

- Future INI-CET session seats, current faculty/leadership, actual resident workload, hostel availability/night travel and final payable package require verification.

## Nuclear Medicine

**Editorial shortlisting view:** Editorial view: local NBEMS-verified NIMS is a practical first enquiry. CMC/Amrita/JIPMER document broad diagnostic and therapeutic services; Hinduja/Apollo are programme-verified DNB comparisons. Cancer-centre alternatives in Delhi/Kolkata may add oncology depth but require proof of general functional-imaging and therapy participation. No degree title alone establishes theranostic competence.

- PET-CT/SPECT/gamma-camera variety and uptime; general functional imaging as well as oncology.
- Radionuclide therapy ward access, radioiodine/PRRT/PSMA availability, admissions and patient follow-up per resident.
- Radiopharmacy, quality control, tracer logistics, dosimetry and radiation-safety training; verify pregnancy/occupational-health arrangements.
- Senior physician on-site supervision, reporting feedback and multidisciplinary meetings; verify external rotations if clinical case mix is narrow.
- NEET-PG/MCC, state counselling and INI-CET are separate routes; verify the actual programme and current-cycle seat/category.
- DNB accredited broad-specialty capacity, post-diploma capacity and live counselling availability are separate quantities.
- No rank, reservation category or domicile eligibility was supplied; this is training due diligence, not an admission prediction.

## Biochemistry

**Editorial shortlisting view:** Editorial view: define the career first. If you want clinical laboratory medicine/quality, CMC’s documented clinical posting, KMC Manipal’s clinical/special-test scope and JIPMER’s quality-review structure deserve targeted enquiry. AIIMS New Delhi has particularly explicit research/teaching preparation, so verify broad routine clinical-lab postings rather than assuming them. Compare NIMS/Osmania locally before accepting a high-fee offer.

- Confirm supervised responsibility for preanalytical errors, IQC/EQAS interpretation, method validation, assay troubleshooting, critical-value communication and consultation, not only undergraduate teaching.
- See actual clinical-laboratory rotation schedule, current test menu/volumes, special chemistry/IEM/toxicology exposure and research mentorship; distinguish MD from MSc/PhD responsibilities.
- Check protected research funding/mentorship, biostatistics, publication ethics and whether students graduate with marketable clinical-quality and interpretation skills.
- Establish how much time is real clinical laboratory work versus undergraduate teaching, and whether the degree fulfils your preferred academic/laboratory career route.
- Only MD Biochemistry is included because that is the requested award.
- AIIMS/JIPMER use INI-CET separately; exact state/minority/deemed admission and bond conditions vary.
- A large automated test count or an NABL logo does not by itself establish MD training responsibility.

**Established:** Department established 1957; MD and clinical investigations began 1965, according to official history. https://jipmer.edu.in/department/biochemistry/general-info

**Resident Welfare:** July 2026 prospectus provides separate male/female hostels, limited rooms subject to availability, primarily for non-Puducherry candidates; HRA is not granted while in hostel. These are accommodation rules, not independent proof of supportive department culture. Ask female residents about night access, duty/rest, leave, grievance handling and timely pay. https://jipmer.edu.in/sites/default/files/3.%20%20Prospectus%20Part%20-%20B%20%28JIPMER%29.pdf

**Cost:** July 2026 MD/MS fee table: tuition₹2,200/year plus₹9,000/year learning resources,₹110/year corpus and₹1,500/year student information (₹12,810 recurring academic charges/year);₹5,000 admission and₹150 ID are one-time, plus₹3,000 refundable caution deposit. Hostel/living costs are separate. Residency contract states Level 10 entry basic₹56,100/month plus applicable allowances, not take-home. Check subsequent-session revisions. https://jipmer.edu.in/sites/default/files/3.%20%20Prospectus%20Part%20-%20B%20%28JIPMER%29.pdf

**Language:** Editorial fit: plan to learn clinical Tamil for local histories/consent. Ask residents how Telugu/Hindi/English are used with referral patients, what interpreter support exists and the expected learning period; do not assume English-only patient work. 

**Travel:** Not established from consulted sources; verify the exact training campus, practical railway station, passenger airport, Hyderabad connections and safe late-arrival transport. No travel time or distance has been estimated. 

**Supplied matrices:** No exact campus-and-course match joined in the supplied files; this does not mean zero seats.

- Use INI-CET eligibility/seat list and Puducherry main-campus prospectus, not MCC or Karaikal-campus assumptions.

**Evidence gaps:**

#### AIIMS New Delhi — Biochemistry — MD

New Delhi, Delhi · Primary · Central government / INI
